108	## 四、P0 集测项（数据通路核心）
109	
110	### T001：端到端 TCP 接收数据通路
111	
112	| 属性 | 内容 |
113	|------|------|
114	| **覆盖 feature** | connection → runtime → receive → expression → display |
115	| **测试类型** | 真 TCP（Node `net` 模块） |
116	| **优先级** | P0 |
117	| **依赖** | 无 |
118	| **测试内容** | 启动 TCP server → composite adapter connect → client 发送测试帧 → routingTick 执行 → receive 解析 → expression 计算 → fanOutToDisplay 更新 |
119	| **验证点** | (1) TCP 字节到达 receive service；(2) 帧匹配成功；(3) 字段解析正确；(4) 表达式求值正确；(5) display selector 反映最新值 |
120	| **oracle 来源** | S004 §2.2 items 1-6（receive 管线行为）；`public/data/frames/configs/*.json` 帧定义 |
121	| **质量规则** | R8（receive/send 主链显式 IO） |
122	| **现有覆盖差距** | connection-network-adapter.spec.ts 有 TCP 先例但只测 adapter 层，未穿透到 receive |
123	
124	### T001b：Send→Receive TCP 回环（帧级 loopback）
125	
126	| 属性 | 内容 |
127	|------|------|
128	| **覆盖 feature** | send → connection → receive → expression → display |
129	| **测试类型** | 真 TCP（Node `net` 模块，echo server） |
130	| **优先级** | P0（最高） |
131	| **依赖** | 无（可与 T001 并行） |
132	| **测试内容** | TCP echo server → send 构帧写出到 TCP → echo 回环 → receive 解析 → 验证发送值 == 接收值 |
133	| **验证点** | (1) **checksum 回环**：send 写 checksum → receive 验证 checksum → 一致；(2) **factor 回环**：send 构帧时 factor 逆换算 → receive 解析时 factor → 原始值还原；(3) **字节序回环**：send 大端写入 → receive 大端读出 → 一致；(4) **expression 回环**：send 时 expression 求值写入 → receive 时 expression 读出 → 值正确；(5) **length field 回环**：send auto length 回填 → receive 用 length 截取 → 字节对齐；(6) 多字段混合帧的完整 round-trip |
134	| **oracle 来源** | S004 §2.2（receive 管线 17 项行为）+ §2.3（send 管线 18 项行为）；帧定义 `public/data/frames/configs/*.json` |
135	| **质量规则** | R8（最核心验证） |
136	| **现有覆盖差距** | 零覆盖。send 和 receive 各自有单测，但从未测过"发出去的能原样收回来" |
137	| **意义** | 这是用户实际使用中最核心的闭环。T001（纯 receive）和 T003（纯 send）是单向验证，T001b 是闭环验证 |
138

681	## 八、测试基础设施约定
682	
683	1. **TCP 测试**：用 Node `net` 模块，参考 `connection-network-adapter.spec.ts` 模式
684	2. **回环测试**：TCP echo server（`net.createServer` + `socket.pipe(socket)`），验证发送字节等于接收字节
685	3. **fake adapter**：复用各 feature 已有的 `createFake*` 工厂
686	4. **测试文件位置**：`rewrite/src/__tests__/integration/`
687	5. **不引入新依赖**：只用 Vitest + Node 内置模块
688	6. **可重复运行**：不依赖外部服务、端口冲突用随机端口
689	7. **fake timers**：task 定时相关测试用 `vi.useFakeTimers()`，避免真实 setTimeout 泄漏
690	
691	---

700	| R10（northbound 独立边界） | T005, T019 | SCOE 链路+旧 JSON 迁移 |

```
1	# [S005] 新系统接缝审计
2	
3	> 2026-05-19 | 调研 | 完成
4	> 9 个 agent 分 3 批并行，覆盖 bridges / adapters / platform / routingTick / service seams / persistence / connection lifecycle / receive pipeline / 现有测试
5	
6	## 目标
7	
8	审计 rewrite 新系统的 runtime 接缝、adapter 边界和持久化时序，识别所有集成级别的断裂风险，产出事实报告。
9	
10	## 记录
11	
12	### 一、高风险接缝（7 项）
13	
14	#### H1. fanOutToStorage 未 await，错误被吞掉
15	
16	- **位置**：`routing-tick.ts:42`
17	- **事实**：`fanOutToStorage(outcomes)` 是 async 函数但调用时未 await，存储失败被静默忽略
18	- **影响**：接收数据可能永远不写入存储，且无任何错误提示
19	- **建议测试**：集成测试中 mock storage 失败，验证错误是否传播
20	
21	#### H2. 事件截断导致永久丢失（EVENT_LIMIT=50）
22	
23	- **位置**：`connection/core/lifecycle.ts:17`、`receive/state/receive-state.ts:184`
24	- **事实**：连接层 `appendEvent` 使用 `slice(-50)` 丢弃旧事件；接收层 `bounded` 使用 `slice(0, 50)` 丢弃新事件
25	- **影响**：高频数据下超过 50 的事件永久丢失，不可恢复
26	- **建议测试**：压力测试，模拟 1000+ 事件/秒，验证截断行为和计数准确性
27	
28	#### H3. LazyPersistence 启动竞态
29	
30	- **位置**：`rewriteRuntime.ts:51-53`
31	- **事实**：`initPersistenceAsync` 是 fire-and-forget，不等待完成。setDelegate 前所有 load() 返回空数据、所有 save*() 静默丢弃
32	- **影响**：用户可能在数据加载完成前操作帧数据，覆盖刚加载的内容；或误以为保存成功
33	- **建议测试**：时序竞争测试，验证 setDelegate 前后的 load/save 行为
34	
35	#### H4. save*() 方法无任何调用方
36	
37	- **位置**：`persistence.ts:70-91`
38	- **事实**：saveFrames/saveConnections/saveSettings/saveAll 已实现，但搜索整个 rewrite/src/ 无任何业务代码调用。无自动保存、无退出钩子、无定时检查点
39	- **影响**：所有运行时修改（帧增删改、连接配置、设置）仅在内存中，应用关闭或崩溃即丢失
40	- **建议测试**：数据丢失回归测试，验证退出/崩溃后数据是否持久化
41	
42	#### H5. TCP Server 事件队列溢出丢弃
43	
44	- **位置**：`network-handlers.ts:86-96`
45	- **事实**：事件队列 maxQueueDepth=100，满时 shift 丢弃最旧事件（包括连接/断开/数据事件）
46	- **影响**：高频连接/断开或大量数据时，关键事件可能被丢弃
47	- **建议测试**：压力测试，快速连接/断开大量 client，验证事件队列行为
48	
49	#### H6. readModel 硬编码为空 Map
50	
51	- **位置**：`processor.ts:269`
52	- **事实**：`readModel: new Map()`，跨帧变量解析功能已实现（expression-pass.ts 208-216 行有 frame_field 查询逻辑）但无法使用
53	- **影响**：跨帧表达式（如 `frame_B.field_x + 1`）永远返回 undefined
54	- **建议测试**：集成测试，验证 readModel 构建和传递后跨帧表达式的正确性
55	
56	#### H7. composite adapter drainEvents() serial 为 null 时 TypeError
57	
58	- **位置**：`composite-adapter.ts:88-99`
59	- **事实**：serialAdapter 为 undefined 时 drainEvents() 直接访问 `.drainEvents()` 会抛 TypeError；connect/write/disconnect 有 null 检查但 drainEvents 没有
60	- **影响**：无串口环境下调用 drainEvents() 崩溃
61	- **建议测试**：边界测试，serialAdapter 为 undefined 时的所有操作
62	
63	### 二、中风险接缝（10 项）
64	
65	#### M1. routingTick 无背压机制
66	
67	- **位置**：`routing-tick.ts` 全链路
68	- **事实**：100ms tick 间隔，无背压机制。一次 drain 产出大量事件时，后续 tick 继续累积，可能内存暴增
69	- **建议测试**：压力测试，模拟 1000+ 事件/秒持续运行
70	
71	#### M2. drainInputSource 同步阻塞
72	
73	- **位置**：`receive-service.ts:230-238`
74	- **事实**：`for...of` 同步遍历事件，每个事件调用 ingest，大量事件时单次 tick 耗时过长
75	- **建议测试**：性能测试，验证大量事件时的 tick 耗时
76	
77	#### M3. receive-storage-bridge / receive-display-bridge 静默失败
78	
79	- **位置**：`receive-storage-bridge.ts:31-32`、`receive-display-bridge.ts:28-29`
80	- **事实**：下游失败时返回 0 但不抛异常、不记录错误信息
81	- **影响**：数据写入存储或显示更新失败时无法感知
82	- **建议测试**：故障注入测试，mock 下游失败，验证错误传播
83	
84	#### M4. receive-event-source-bridge 处理器无异常隔离
85	
86	- **位置**：`receive-event-source-bridge.ts:17-19`
87	- **事实**：单个处理器异常会中断全部处理器执行
88	- **建议测试**：异常隔离测试
89	
90	#### M5. connection-backed-writer 事件依赖脆弱
91	
92	- **位置**：`connection-backed-writer.ts:30-32`
93	- **事实**：依赖 `find()` 查找 write-accepted 事件，事件缺失时可能返回 0 字节数
94	- **建议测试**：事件缺失场景测试
95	
96	#### M6. Preload 类型推断不一致
97	
98	- **位置**：`preload/index.ts:35-43, 60-68`
99	- **事实**：connect 时依赖返回值推断类型，事件监听依赖 `target.role` 推断，两者可能不同步。connect 返回成功前事件先到达时类型推断会失败
100	- **建议测试**：竞态测试，connect 和事件同时到达
101	
102	#### M7. connections/settings 启动恢复缺失
103	
104	- **位置**：`rewriteRuntime.ts:58-81`
105	- **事实**：frames 能恢复，connections 和 settings 完全未实现恢复。ConnectionService 无批量替换 API
106	- **影响**：用户每次启动需重新配置连接和设置
107	- **建议测试**：恢复完整性测试
108	
109	#### M8. autoConnect 配置存在但逻辑缺失
110	
111	- **位置**：`types.ts:60`
112	- **事实**：`autoConnect?: boolean` 配置已定义，UI 有开关，但 connection-service 中完全不检查此属性
113	- **影响**：用户开启自动连接但不生效
114	- **建议测试**：配置驱动集成测试
115	
116	#### M9. 重连定时器潜在泄漏
117	
118	- **位置**：`connection-service.ts:248`
119	- **事实**：reconnect 定时器使用 Map 管理，长时间运行+多次重连可能导致泄漏
120	- **建议测试**：长时间运行内存泄漏测试
121	
122	#### M10. 字段键冲突（无来源区分）
123	
124	- **位置**：`receive-state.ts:118-119`
125	- **事实**：`fieldKey` 只用 `frameId:fieldId`，同帧同字段多次到达会覆盖，无来源区分
126	- **建议测试**：多源并发接收测试
127	
128	### 三、低风险接缝（5 项）
129	
130	| 编号 | 接缝 | 位置 | 说明 |
131	|------|------|------|------|
132	| L1 | composite adapter 路由覆盖 | composite-adapter.ts:10-26 | 路由完全覆盖所有 TransportKind，无风险 |
133	| L2 | TCP server/client 模式差异 | real-network-adapter.ts:20-38 | 配置相同，错误语义正确区分 |
134	| L3 | UDP remote 校验 | real-network-adapter.ts:119-141 | 提前验证，错误信息清晰，已有测试 |
135	| L4 | 表达式错误传播 | processor.ts:240-314 | 错误传播路径清晰，标准化错误码，降级处理合理 |
136	| L5 | Platform facade 缓存 | platform/index.ts:46-58 | 单例缓存模式一致，有手动清除机制 |
137	
138	### 四、现有集成测试覆盖审计
139	
140	**已有覆盖（3 个 spec 文件）**：
141	- `feature-wiring.spec.ts`：桥接器单元级数据转换测试
142	- `routing-tick.spec.ts`：单次 tick 成功/失败/空事件路径
143	- `bootstrap-integration.spec.ts`：端到端 runtime 创建 → 连接 → 推数据 → tick
144	
145	**mock 与真实行为差异**：
146	- Fake adapter 同步立即返回，真实 adapter 有 I/O 延迟
147	- Mock service 返回空快照，真实 service 维护可变状态和副作用
148	- 未测试 tick 执行期间连接断开、大量数据积压、长时间运行的资源清理
149	
150	**分界线**：现有测试覆盖了"单个 tick 的同步控制流和数据格式"；缺失"多次 tick 的异步状态管理、真实 I/O 交互、跨 feature 数据一致性"。
```

```
14	
15	# Rewrite target structure
16	
17	## 1. Purpose
18	
19	本文定义全面重写时的目标目录结构、职责归口和依赖方向。
20	
21	本文不做以下事情：
22	
23	- 不定义字段 schema。
24	- 不写接口契约。
25	- 不制定迁移批次。
26	- 不要求一次性创建所有空目录。
27	- 不把旧代码热点直接翻译成新目录。
28	
29	本文继承当前范围口径：旧功能默认保留，重写重点是代码质量、模块边界、状态归口和 Electron 边界。`codestable/compound/2026-04-28-rewrite-scope-default-preserve.md`
30	
31	## Batch 0 Architecture Baseline
32	
33	本文是 Batch 0 的整体架构基线。后续每个功能域进入 `cs-feat-design` 前，都必须把本文列为直接合同或边界护栏，并说明本批如何遵守本文的目录、依赖、状态和平台边界。
34	
35	Batch 0 只固定后续功能必须遵守的架构骨架：
36	
37	- 顶层目录固定为 `app / platform / shared / features / runtime / pages / widgets`。
38	- feature 内部通用分层固定为 `core / services / state / adapters / composables / components / fixtures`，按实际职责创建，不为了填满模板而建空层。
39	- feature public API 只是跨 feature 访问边界，不是必须新增的接口层；外部不得 import 其他 feature 的内部 `state`、`adapters`、内部 composable、内部 service 实现或私有 helper。
40	- `runtime/` 只在应用生命周期、平台资源装配或跨 feature 编排确有需要时出现，不是新的全局业务中心。
41	- renderer、platform、preload、main 的边界按本文和质量规则执行；现有 main 业务化只作为迁移风险处理，不作为新架构目标。
42	- 状态必须分清静态资产、运行事实、统计 read model 和 UI snapshot；运行统计不得写回 frame definition、字段定义或其他静态资产。
43	- 高频数据按 batch、delta、队列、节流、snapshot 和背压处理，页面不直接消费底层逐包事件。
44	- 跨 feature 通信只允许公开 service、runtime 编排、显式事件或边界输入输出，不允许互相 import 内部 state。
45	- pages、widgets、feature components 只承担各自 UI 边界，不拥有协议语义、任务推进、平台访问或跨域流程。
46	- 测试、fixture 和 oracle 必须放在可复用、可审查的位置，优先归入对应 feature 的 `fixtures/` 或 `core` 测试材料。
47	
48	Batch 0 不固定以下内容：
49	
50	- 不写字段 schema、接口签名、事件 payload 或 northbound 字段。
51	- 不设计 receive、send、task、SCOE、result、report、northbound 的内部算法和流程细节。
52	- 不给高频链路写具体队列深度、窗口大小或延迟阈值；这些参数必须在对应 feature design 中结合目标硬件、runtime 证据或压测计划确定。
53	- 不为每个 feature 预先创建 public API 清单；只有出现外部消费者或跨 feature 协作时，才在对应 `cs-feat-design` 中定义允许入口。
54	
55	## 2. Existing Boundary Inputs
56	
57	Evidence:
58	
59	- 桌面能力需要统一收口；页面、组件、Store、composables 不应直接把 Electron 原生对象当正式业务入口。`codestable/architecture/boundary-desktop-capability-access.md:49-53`, `codestable/architecture/boundary-desktop-capability-access.md:165-167`
60	- 运行主状态必须区分主状态、派生态、展示态、记录态和缓存态，主状态不得多点写入，下游不得用共享状态回读替代明确边界输入。`codestable/architecture/boundary-runtime-state-ownership.md:124-126`, `codestable/architecture/boundary-runtime-state-ownership.md:148-150`, `codestable/architecture/boundary-runtime-state-ownership.md:223-224`
61	- 接收主链和发送主链都只能处理显式输入 / 输出，不能成为统一运行主状态或中心任务本体。`codestable/architecture/topology-receive-send-mainlines.md:179-181`, `codestable/architecture/topology-receive-send-mainlines.md:302-304`, `codestable/architecture/topology-receive-send-mainlines.md:524-525`
62	- 任务系统是通用执行引擎，接收执行计划（step 序列）按顺序执行、处理错误、追踪进度；触发来源（用户 UI、定时器、receive 触发、SCOE 命令、northbound 外部命令）通过入口适配区分，执行逻辑统一。任务系统不等于页面工作台或本地发送对象。`codestable/architecture/domain-ta[REDACTED_SK].md:93-99`, `codestable/architecture/domain-ta[REDACTED_SK].md:192-223`
63	- SCOE 更适合被视为领域模块，必须通过显式边界接入，不得作为通用接收链或发送链内部长期特判。`codestable/architecture/domain-scoe-position.md:562-575`, `codestable/architecture/topology-receive-send-mainlines.md:386-394`
64	- 北向协同应由独立边界层统一承接接入、投影和交付，不能直接落到任务系统、统一运行主状态或收发主链上。`codestable/architecture/boundary-northbound-collaboration-delivery.md:91-117`, `codestable/architecture/boundary-northbound-collaboration-delivery.md:509-516`
65	
66	Update:
67	
68	- 早期桌面能力文档讨论过受控开启 `nodeIntegration` 的备选。当前重写口径已经收紧为 `nodeIntegration: false`、`contextIsolation: true`、`sandbox: false`。本文以后者为准，同时继承“桌面能力统一收口”这个核心原则。
69	
70	## 3. Rewrite root placement
71	
72	本轮固定新重写工程落点：
73	
74	- 仓库根目录下的 `rewrite/` 是新的独立应用根目录，不只是源码目录。
75	- 新 renderer 源码根是 `rewrite/src/`。
76	- 新 Electron 边界从 `rewrite/src-electron/main/` 和 `rewrite/src-electron/preload/` 开始。
77	- 旧 `src/`、`src-electron/`、`public/` 只作为旧系统 evidence、oracle 或 migration input，不作为新代码落点。
78	- 旧 `src/api/common`、旧 `window.electron` 大包、旧 preload 聚合和旧 main business handlers 不搬入 `rewrite/`。
79	
80	独立应用根目录允许承载自己的基础设施文件，例如 `package.json`、`quasar.config.ts`、`tsconfig.json`、`eslint.config.js`、`uno.config.ts` 和测试配置。当前仓库根配置只能作为可复用依赖和配置思路的 evidence，不能原样升级为新工程基线。
```

```
14	
15	# Rewrite quality rules
16	
17	## 1. Purpose
18	
19	本文定义全面重写阶段的质量红线。
20	
21	这些规则服务于一个目标：旧功能默认保留，但旧代码组织、旧全局状态穿透、旧模块耦合和旧 Electron 暴露方式不保留。
22	
23	本文不定义字段 schema，不写接口契约，不制定迁移批次，也不裁剪旧功能范围。功能范围以前置范围文档为准：`codestable/compound/2026-04-28-rewrite-scope-default-preserve.md`。
24	
25	## 2. Source Documents
26	
27	必须继承：
28	
29	- `codestable/compound/2026-04-28-rewrite-scope-default-preserve.md`
30	- `codestable/architecture/rewrite-target-structure.md`
31	- `codestable/quality/rewrite-validation-fixture-oracle-baseline.md`
32	- `easysdd/compound/2026-04-27-legacy-feature-inventory-and-oracle-map.md`
33	
34	可作为证据来源：
35	
36	- `codestable/architecture/boundary-runtime-state-ownership.md`
37	- `codestable/architecture/topology-receive-send-mainlines.md`
38	- `codestable/architecture/domain-ta[REDACTED_SK].md`
39	- `codestable/architecture/domain-scoe-position.md`
40	- `codestable/architecture/boundary-northbound-collaboration-delivery.md`
41	- `codestable/compound/2026-04-24-project-quality-conventions-system-analysis.md`
42	
43	## 3. Rule Status
44	
45	| Status | Meaning |
46	| --- | --- |
47	| MUST | 违反后不得合入重写主线。 |
48	| SHOULD | 默认遵守；若例外，必须写明理由和替代验证。 |
49	| MAY | 可选做法，不作为门禁。 |
50	
51	## 4. Hard Rules
52	
53	### R1. Preserve behavior, replace structure
54	
55	MUST:
56	
57	- 默认保留旧系统用户可见能力。
58	- 只在明确低价值、高成本、冲突或旧占位时，才把功能列入排除候选。
59	- 将旧功能重写到新的边界中，而不是把旧目录、旧 store 和旧 IPC 复制一遍。
60	
61	MUST NOT:
62	
63	- 因为质量规则而顺手砍旧功能。
64	- 把“旧代码很脏”理解成“旧行为不重要”。
65	- 把未讨论的功能降级成 future work。
66	
67	### R2. Feature ownership must be explicit
68	
69	MUST:
70	
71	- 按 `features/frame`、`connection`、`receive`、`send`、`task`、`scoe`、`storage`、`settings`、`status`、`result`、`report`、`northbound` 归口业务能力。
72	- 每个 feature 只能直接修改自己的状态。
73	- 跨 feature 流程必须通过公开 service、runtime 编排、显式事件或边界输入输出发生。
74	- 每个 feature 的外部入口必须明确；外部只能通过 public API 调用，不直接依赖内部实现。
75	
76	MUST NOT:
77	
78	- feature A 直接 import feature B 的内部 `state` 并修改。
79	- feature A 直接 import feature B 的内部 service、adapter、composable 或私有 helper。
80	- 用一个大 store 同时承载主状态、展示态、缓存态、任务调度和多下游副作用。
81	- 让页面组件长期持有运行主状态或任务生命周期事实。
82	
83	### R3. Core logic must be testable TypeScript
84	
85	MUST:
86	
87	- 将 receive 解析、send 构帧、SCOE 协议、task 状态推进、storage 迁移、result/report 生成等核心规则放在可测试的 TypeScript 层。
88	- `features/*/core` 不依赖 Vue、Pinia、Electron、`window.electron`、`ipcRenderer`、`fs`、`serialport`。
89	- 核心逻辑通过明确输入和输出表达行为。
90	
91	MUST NOT:
92	
93	- 把解析、构帧、协议校验、任务推进写在 Vue 组件里。
94	- 在 core 逻辑里读取全局 store 补上下文。
95	- 在 core 逻辑里直接访问平台能力。
96	
97	### R4. UI is not the business workflow owner
98	
99	MUST:
100	
```

我已经读取了所有相关的设计文档。现在将提取与集成测试项 T004、T005、T012、T013、T016 相关的验收标准和跨功能交互契约。

## 集成测试验收标准提取报告

### T004（条件匹配 → task → send）

**接收输出触发任务执行：**
- runtime routingTick 将 connection data 事件经 receive 解析后触发 task 条件匹配
- routingTick 流程：drain connection → feed receive → extract matches → notify task

**receive 到 task 的桥接：**
- `ReceiveEventSourceBridge` 实现 task 的 `ReceiveEventSource` 接口
- Bridge 输入：`emit(inputs: ConditionMatchInput[])`，通知所有订阅的 handler
- Bridge subscribe 返回 unsubscribe 函数

**ConditionMatchInput 格式：**
```typescript
interface ConditionMatchInput {
  readonly frameId: string;
  readonly sourceId?: string;
  readonly fieldValues: Readonly<Record<string, number | string | null>>;
}
```
提取规则：遍历 receive outcome 中 kind='matched' 的每条，遍历每个字段，生成一个 input（frameId + fieldId + value + sourceId）

### T005（command-ingress → task → send → ACK）

**SCOE 协议帧格式：**
- 功能码验证：4字节功能码（scoeIdentifier + commandCode + 0xAA0xAA）
- 校验和验证：sum % 256，支持多个 checksum 配置
- 参数提取：offset/length → hex → receiveCode 匹配 → option.value

**command-ingress 创建 task：**
- SEND_FRAME → TaskDefinition 翻译：
  - schedule: { kind: 'immediate' }（顺序执行所有步骤）
  - 每个 frameMapping → 一个 send step
  - userFieldValues 从 resolvedParams 填充
  - 完成条件 → wait-condition step
- READ_FILE_AND_SEND → TaskDefinition 翻译：
  - schedule: { kind: 'timer', intervalMs }
  - 使用 fieldVariations
  - stopCondition.maxIterations = fileLines.length

**ACK 帧构造：**
- 通过 `sendService.execute()` 发送确认帧
- onSettled 回调：task 完成后发送 successFrameId
- SendRequest：{ frameId, targetId, userFieldValues: {} }

### T012（timer driver）

**ScheduleDriver 类型：**
```typescript
type ScheduleDriver =
  | { readonly kind: 'immediate' }
  | { readonly kind: 'timer'; readonly intervalMs: number }
  | { readonly kind: 'event'; readonly conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[]; readonly cooldownMs?: number }
```

**timer 驱动实现：**
- 首次调用无延迟
- 后续调用等待 intervalMs
- 通过 signal 支持中断

**与 task 生命周期集成：**
- 驱动 waitForNext 返回 boolean（true 继续，false 终止）
- dispose 时清理定时器
- 支持暂停/恢复（清除/重建定时器）

### T013（event driver）

**event driver vs timer driver：**
- timer：固定间隔执行
- event：条件触发执行，支持 cooldown

**条件匹配逻辑：**
- `ConditionTerm` 扩展支持 `logicOperator`（'and' | 'or'）
- `evaluateConditionGroup` 支持 AND/OR 组合和短路
- `ConditionRegistry.processInput` 按 frameId 索引查找条件组

**Cooldown 和 maxTriggerCount：**
- cooldownMs：上次触发后冷却时间
- maxTriggerCount：最大触发次数（通过 stopCondition.maxIterations 控制）

### T016（routingTick 错误隔离）

**bridge 顺序调用：**
- routingTick 中 eventConsumers 链式调用
- command-ingress 先消费，remaining 交给 receive

**错误处理：**
- routingTick 每步检查 outcome.ok
- 失败时返回 {ok: false, error}，跳过后续步骤
- 不做重试、退避或断路

---

## 跨功能交互契约总结

| 测试项 | 涉及 feature | 交互契约 | 数据格式 |
|--------|-------------|---------|---------|
| T004 | receive→task→send | receive 产出 matched outcome → bridge.emit(ConditionMatchInput[]) → task 条件匹配 → send 执行 | ConditionMatchInput: {frameId, sourceId?, fieldValues} |
| T005 | command-ingress→task→send | SCOE 解析 → TaskDefinition → taskService.createTask/startTask → onSettled → sendService.execute(ACK) | TaskDefinition: {schedule, steps, fieldVariations?, stopCondition?} |
| T012 | task runtime | ScheduleDriver.timer → waitForNext → executeSteps → check stopCondition | intervalMs, maxIterations |
| T013 | task runtime | ScheduleDriver.event → registerGroup → waitForNext → cooldown 检查 | conditions: ConditionTerm[], cooldownMs |
| T016 | runtime | eventConsumers 链式调用 → 每步检查 outcome.ok → 失败即停 | RoutingTickResult: {ok, error?, eventsRouted, matchesEmitted} |

## 文件结构分析报告

基于对18个文件的完整读取，以下是编写 Batch 3 (T004, T005, T012, T013, T016) 集成测试所需的关键结构总结：

---

### 1. **routing-tick.ts** - 核心路由逻辑
**导出：**
- `RoutingTickResult` 接口：`{ ok: boolean, error?: string, eventsRouted: number, matchesEmitted: number }`
- `routingTick(features: RewriteWiredFeatures): Promise<RoutingTickResult>`

**核心流程：**
1. `connectionService.drainAdapterEvents()` - 获取连接事件
2. 过滤 `kind === 'data' && event.bytes !== undefined` 的事件
3. `receiveService.drainInputSource(source)` - 处理接收数据
4. `fanOutToDisplay()` - 同步分发到显示
5. `await fanOutToStorage()` - 异步分发到存储
6. `receiveEventSourceBridge.emit(matchInputs)` - 发送条件匹配输入

**依赖关系：** 连接 → 接收 → 显示/存储 → 任务桥接

---

### 2. **receive-event-source-bridge.ts** - 桥接层
**导出：**
- `ReceiveEventSourceBridge` 类实现 `ReceiveEventSource` 接口

**方法签名：**
- `subscribe(handler: (input: ConditionMatchInput) => void): () => void`
- `emit(inputs: readonly ConditionMatchInput[]): void`
- `clear(): void`

**关键类型：**
```typescript
interface ConditionMatchInput {
  readonly frameId: string;
  readonly fieldValues: Readonly<Record<string, number | string | null>>;
  readonly sourceId?: string;
}
```

---

### 3. **receive-display-bridge.ts** - 显示分发
**导出：**
- `fanOutToDisplay(service: DisplayService, outcomes: readonly ReceiveBatchOutcome[]): number`

**转换逻辑：**
- 仅处理 `kind === 'matched'` 的 outcome
- 转换为 `DisplayFieldMaterial[]`：`{ groupId, dataItemId, fieldName, value, displayValue, updatedAt }`
- 返回成功分发的字段数量

---

### 4. **receive-storage-bridge.ts** - 存储分发
**导出：**
- `fanOutToStorage(service: StorageLocalService, outcomes: readonly ReceiveBatchOutcome[]): Promise<number>`

**转换逻辑：**
- 过滤条件：`kind === 'matched' && outcome.input && outcome.fields.length > 0`
- 转换为 `StorageLocalRecord[]`：`{ id, capturedAt, source, channel, fields[] }`
- 返回写入的记录数量

---

### 5. **task-service.ts** - 任务服务核心
**导出接口：**
```typescript
interface TaskReader {
  getSnapshot(): TaskStateSnapshot;
  getInstance(instanceId: string): TaskInstanceState | undefined;
  getProgress(instanceId: string): TaskProgress | undefined;
  getStatistics(): TaskStatisticsSnapshot;
}

interface TaskService extends TaskReader {
  createTask(definition: TaskDefinition): TaskInstanceState;
  startTask(instanceId: string): void;
  pauseTask(instanceId: string): void;
  resumeTask(instanceId: string): void;
  stopTask(instanceId: string): void;
  stopAll(): number;
  removeTask(instanceId: string): void;
  retryTask(sourceInstanceId: string): TaskInstanceState | undefined;
  updateTask(instanceId: string, definition: TaskDefinition): TaskInstanceState | undefined;
  onSettled(instanceId: string): Promise<void>;
}
```

**工厂函数：**
- `createTaskService(options: CreateTaskServiceOptions): TaskService`
- 选项包括：`sendService`, `receiveEventSource`, `timerService`, `fieldValueProvider`, `state`, `now`

**内部机制：**
- 订阅管理：`ensureSubscription()`, `releaseSubscription()`
- 执行循环：`runExecutionLoop(instanceId, signal)` - 异步 fire-and-forget
- 生命周期管理器、步执行器、错误策略处理器、迭代循环

---

### 6. **task/core/types.ts** - 任务类型定义
**关键类型：**

**ScheduleDriver（调度驱动器）：**
```typescript
type ScheduleDriver =
  | { readonly kind: 'immediate' }
  | { readonly kind: 'timer'; readonly intervalMs: number }
  | { readonly kind: 'event'; readonly conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[]; readonly cooldownMs?: number };
```

**ConditionTerm（条件术语）：**
```typescript
interface ConditionTerm {
  readonly frameId: string;
  readonly fieldId: string;
  readonly operator: ComparisonOperator; // eq, neq, gt, lt, gte, lte, contains, change, any
  readonly threshold: string | number;
  readonly sourceId?: string;
  readonly logicOperator?: 'and' | 'or';
}
```

**TaskStepDefinition（步定义）：**
```typescript
type TaskStepDefinition =
  | { readonly kind: 'send'; readonly id: string; readonly config: SendStepConfig }
  | { readonly kind: 'wait-condition'; readonly id: string; readonly config: WaitConditionConfig }
  | { readonly kind: 'delay'; readonly id: string; readonly config: DelayStepConfig };
```

**TaskDefinition（任务定义）：**
```typescript
interface TaskDefinition {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly name: string;
  readonly steps: readonly TaskStepDefinition[];
  readonly schedule: ScheduleDriver;
  readonly stopCondition?: TaskStopCondition;
  readonly fieldVariations?: readonly FieldVariation[];
  readonly errorPolicy: TaskErrorPolicy;
}
```

**TaskInstanceState（任务实例状态）：**
```typescript
interface TaskInstanceState {
  readonly instanceId: string;
  readonly definitionRef: TaskDefinition;
  readonly lifecycle: TaskLifecycleStatus; // created, running, paused, stopped, completed, failed
  readonly currentStepIndex: number;
  readonly currentIteration: number;
  readonly stepResults: readonly TaskStepResult[];
  // ... timestamps
}
```

**常量：**
- `TASK_STEP_KINDS`, `COMPARISON_OPERATORS`, `TASK_ERROR_ACTIONS`, `TASK_LIFECYCLE_STATUSES`

**纯函数：**
- `resolveStopCondition(def: TaskDefinition): ResolvedStopCondition`
- `isStepResultFailed(result: TaskStepResult): boolean`
- `isTerminal(status: TaskLifecycleStatus): boolean`
- `calculateProgress(instance: TaskInstanceState): TaskProgress`

### 8. **send-service.ts** - 发送服务
**导出接口：**
```typescript
interface SendReader {
  getSnapshot(): SendStateSnapshot;
  getStatistics(): SendStatisticsSnapshot;
  listResults(): SendResult[];
  getStatus(): SendServiceStatus;
}

interface SendService extends SendReader {
  execute(request: SendRequest): Promise<SendResult>;
  resetStats(): SendStateSnapshot;
}
```

**SendRequest（发送请求）：**
```typescript
interface SendRequest {
  readonly frameId: string;
  readonly targetId: string;
  readonly userFieldValues?: Readonly<Record<string, string | number | boolean>>;
  readonly fieldValues?: Readonly<Record<string, string | number | boolean>>;
  readonly variables?: VariableMap;
  readonly context: SendContext;
  readonly options?: SendOptions;
}
```

**SendResult（发送结果）：**
```typescript
type SendResultKind = 'sent' | 'build-error' | 'target-unavailable' | 'transport-error' | 'timeout';

interface SendResult {
  readonly kind: SendResultKind;
  readonly requestRef: SendRequestRef;
  readonly bytesBuilt: number;
  readonly bytesSent: number;
  readonly timestamp: string;
  readonly error?: SendError;
  readonly buildIssues: readonly SendBuildIssue[];
}
```

**9步执行流程：**
1. 验证请求（frameId, targetId, context.source）
2. 解析帧快照
3. 方向检查（不能发送 receive-only 帧）
4. 转换为编码定义 + 解析字段值
5. 构建帧字节
6. 应用后构建补丁（校验和、长度字段）
7. 解析目标
8. 传输写入
9. 成功/失败结果

### 10. **command-ingress-service.ts** - 命令入口服务

```typescript
interface CommandIngressService {
```

6. **使用辅助函数** - `dataEvent`, `matchedOutcome`, `okOutcome` 构建测试数据
7. **测试错误场景** - 使用 `failOutcome`, 模拟服务失败
8. **验证扇出** - 监控 `fanOutToDisplay`, `fanOutToStorage` 的调用
9. **验证桥接** - 监控 `ReceiveEventSourceBridge.emit` 的调用
10. **测试多源** - 使用多个连接验证 fieldKey 冲突行为

- **T004**: 任务事件调度（timer/event 驱动）

- **T013**: 命令入口任务跟踪（onSettled, ACK 帧）
- **T016**: 多源接收 + 出站路由

这些文件提供了完整的类型定义、服务接口和测试模式，可以用于编写全面的集成测试。

```
1	import type { ConditionMatchInput } from '@/features/task';
2	import { ConnectionToReceiveInputSource } from './bridges/connection-to-receive';
3	import { fanOutToDisplay } from './bridges/receive-display-bridge';
4	import { fanOutToStorage } from './bridges/receive-storage-bridge';
5	import type { RewriteWiredFeatures } from './feature-wiring';
6	
7	export interface RoutingTickResult {
8	  readonly ok: boolean;
9	  readonly error?: string;
10	  readonly eventsRouted: number;
11	  readonly matchesEmitted: number;
12	}
13	
14	export async function routingTick(
15	  features: RewriteWiredFeatures,
16	): Promise<RoutingTickResult> {
17	  const drainOutcome =
18	    await features.connectionService.drainAdapterEvents();
19	
20	  if (!drainOutcome.ok) {
21	    return {
22	      ok: false,
23	      error: drainOutcome.error?.message ?? 'Connection drain failed',
24	      eventsRouted: 0,
25	      matchesEmitted: 0,
26	    };
27	  }
28	
29	  const dataEvents = drainOutcome.events.filter(
30	    (event) => event.kind === 'data' && event.bytes !== undefined,
31	  );
32	
33	  if (dataEvents.length === 0) {
34	    return { ok: true, eventsRouted: 0, matchesEmitted: 0 };
35	  }
36	
37	  const source = new ConnectionToReceiveInputSource(dataEvents);
38	  const receiveOutcome =
39	    await features.receiveService.drainInputSource(source);
40	
41	  fanOutToDisplay(features.displayService, receiveOutcome.outcomes);
42	  await fanOutToStorage(features.storageService, receiveOutcome.outcomes);
43	
44	  const matchInputs: ConditionMatchInput[] = [];
45	  for (const outcome of receiveOutcome.outcomes) {
46	    if (outcome.kind !== 'matched') continue;
47	    if (!outcome.matchedFrame) continue;
48	
49	    const sourceId = outcome.input?.source.sourceId;
50	    const fieldValues: Record<string, number | string | null> = {};
51	    for (const field of outcome.fields) {
52	      fieldValues[field.fieldId] = field.value as number | string | null;
53	    }
54	    matchInputs.push({
55	      frameId: outcome.matchedFrame.frameId,
56	      fieldValues,
57	      ...(sourceId ? { sourceId } : {}),
58	    });
59	  }
60	
61	  if (matchInputs.length > 0) {
62	    features.receiveEventSourceBridge.emit(matchInputs);
63	  }
64	
65	  return {
66	    ok: true,
67	    eventsRouted: dataEvents.length,
68	    matchesEmitted: matchInputs.length,
69	  };
70	}
71	
```

```
1	/**
2	 * T003: End-to-end task → send execution chain.
3	 *
4	 * Verifies the complete lifecycle: TaskDefinition created → taskService.startTask
5	 * → send step executes → sendService.execute called → transport write occurs →

7	 *
8	 * Verification points:

10	 * 2. send pipeline 9-step executes correctly
11	 * 3. SendResult returned and task progress updated
12	 * 4. Selector reflects task terminal state
13	 * 5. Written bytes match expected frame structure

30	  fields: [
31	    {

35	      length: 1,
36	      inputType: 'input',

46	      length: 2,
47	      inputType: 'input',

49	      options: [],

62	function setupFeatures(): RewriteWiredFeatures {
63	  const adapter = createFakeConnectionTransportAdapter();
64	  const features = wireFeatures({ connectionAdapter: adapter });
65	
66	  // Add frame definition
67	  features.frameService.upsertFrame(sendFrame);

69	  return features;

72	async function connectAndGetTarget(features: RewriteWiredFeatures): Promise<string> {
73	  const connectResult = await features.connectionService.connect({
74	    kind: 'tcp-client',
75	    id: 't003-conn',
76	    host: '127.0.0.1',
77	    port: 9999,
78	  });
79	  expect(connectResult.ok).toBe(true);
80	
81	  const targets = features.connectionService.listTransportTargets();
82	  expect(targets.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
83	
84	  return targets[0]!.targetId;
85	}

87	function createImmediateSendTask(targetId: string): TaskDefinition {
88	  return {
89	    id: 't003-task',
90	    name: 'T003 Immediate Send Task',
91	    schedule: { kind: 'immediate' },
92	    steps: [
93	      {
94	        kind: 'send',
95	        id: 'step-1',

100	          userFieldValues: { payload: 0x1234 },
101	        },
102	      },
103	    ],
104	    errorPolicy: { onStepError: 'stop' },
105	  };
106	}

256	        {

258	          id: 'step-1',
259	          name: 'Long delay',
260	          config: { durationMs: 10000 },
261	        },
262	        {
263	          kind: 'send',
264	          id: 'step-2',
265	          name: 'Send after delay',
266	          config: {
267	            frameId: sendFrame.id,
268	            targetId,
269	          },
270	        },
271	      ],
272	      errorPolicy: { onStepError: 'stop' },
273	    };
274	
275	    const instance = features.taskService.createTask(taskDef);
276	    features.taskService.startTask(instance.instanceId);
277	
278	    // Stop immediately while delay is running
279	    features.taskService.stopTask(instance.instanceId);
280	
281	    const finalInstance = features.taskService.getInstance(instance.instanceId);
282	    expect(finalInstance?.lifecycle).toBe('stopped');
283	  });
284	
285	  it('task can be removed after reaching terminal state', async () => {
286	    const targetId = await connectAndGetTarget(features);
287	    const taskDef = createImmediateSendTask(targetId);
288	
289	    const instance = features.taskService.createTask(taskDef);
290	    features.taskService.startTask(instance.instanceId);
291	    await features.taskService.onSettled(instance.instanceId);
292	
293	    expect(features.taskService.getInstance(instance.instanceId)?.lifecycle).toBe('completed');
294	
295	    // Remove completed task
296	    features.taskService.removeTask(instance.instanceId);
297	    expect(features.taskService.getInstance(instance.instanceId)).toBeUndefined();
298	  });
299	});
300
```

```
1	import type { ConditionMatchInput, ReceiveEventSource } from '@/features/task';
2	
3	export class ReceiveEventSourceBridge implements ReceiveEventSource {
4	  private readonly handlers = new Set<
5	    (input: ConditionMatchInput) => void
6	  >();
7	
8	  subscribe(handler: (input: ConditionMatchInput) => void): () => void {
9	    this.handlers.add(handler);
10	    return () => {
11	      this.handlers.delete(handler);
12	    };
13	  }
14	
15	  emit(inputs: readonly ConditionMatchInput[]): void {
16	    for (const input of inputs) {
17	      for (const handler of this.handlers) {
18	        handler(input);
19	      }
20	    }
21	  }
22	
23	  clear(): void {
24	    this.handlers.clear();
25	  }
26	}
27	
```

```
1	import type { SendResult } from '@/features/send';
2	import type { ReadonlyDeep } from '@/shared/types/readonly-deep';
3	import type { VariableMap } from '@/shared/expression/types';
4	
5	// --- Step kinds ---
6	
7	export const TASK_STEP_KINDS = ['send', 'wait-condition', 'delay'] as const;
8	export type TaskStepKind = (typeof TASK_STEP_KINDS)[number];
9	
10	// --- Comparison ---
11	
12	export const COMPARISON_OPERATORS = [
13	  'eq',
14	  'neq',
15	  'gt',
16	  'lt',
17	  'gte',
18	  'lte',
19	  'contains',
20	  'change',
21	  'any',
22	] as const;
23	export type ComparisonOperator = (typeof COMPARISON_OPERATORS)[number];
24	
25	// --- ConditionTerm (replaces WaitCondition, adds logicOperator) ---
26	
27	export interface ConditionTerm {
28	  readonly frameId: string;
29	  readonly fieldId: string;
30	  readonly operator: ComparisonOperator;
31	  readonly threshold: string | number;
32	  readonly sourceId?: string;
33	  readonly logicOperator?: 'and' | 'or'; // default 'and'; first term's logicOperator is ignored
34	}
35	
36	// --- ScheduleDriver (replaces TaskSchedulingMode + flat scheduling fields) ---
37	
38	export type ScheduleDriver =
39	  | { readonly kind: 'immediate' }
40	  | { readonly kind: 'timer'; readonly intervalMs: number }
41	  | { readonly kind: 'event'; readonly conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[]; readonly cooldownMs?: number };
42	
43	// --- FieldVariation ---
44	
45	export interface FieldVariation {
46	  readonly fieldId: string;
47	  readonly values: readonly (string | number)[];
48	}
49	
50	// --- StepRepeat ---
51	
52	export interface StepRepeat {
53	  readonly intervalMs: number;
54	  readonly until?: readonly ConditionTerm[];
55	  readonly maxCount?: number;
56	}
57	
58	// --- Step definitions ---
59	
60	export interface SendStepConfig {
61	  readonly frameId: string;
62	  readonly targetId: string; // required (was optional + fallback to definition.targetId)
63	  readonly userFieldValues?: Readonly<Record<string, string | number | boolean>>;
64	  readonly variables?: VariableMap;
65	  readonly intervalAfterMs?: number;
66	  readonly repeat?: StepRepeat;
67	}
68	
69	export interface WaitConditionConfig {
70	  readonly conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[]; // single condition -> condition array
71	  readonly timeoutMs?: number; // optional (undefined = wait indefinitely until signal interrupt)
72	  readonly onTimeout: 'continue' | 'skip' | 'fail';
73	}
74	
75	export interface DelayStepConfig {
76	  readonly durationMs: number;
77	}
78	
79	// --- Unified TaskStepDefinition (config field name unified) ---
80	
81	export type TaskStepDefinition =
82	  | { readonly kind: 'send'; readonly id: string; readonly name?: string; readonly config: SendStepConfig }
83	  | { readonly kind: 'wait-condition'; readonly id: string; readonly name?: string; readonly config: WaitConditionConfig }
84	  | { readonly kind: 'delay'; readonly id: string; readonly name?: string; readonly config: DelayStepConfig };
85	
86	// --- Error policy ---
87	
88	export const TASK_ERROR_ACTIONS = ['retry', 'skip-step', 'stop', 'pause'] as const;
89	export type TaskErrorAction = (typeof TASK_ERROR_ACTIONS)[number];
90	
91	export interface TaskErrorPolicy {
92	  readonly onFailure: TaskErrorAction;
93	  readonly retryCount?: number;
94	  readonly retryDelayMs?: number;
95	}
96	
97	// --- Stop condition ---
98	
99	export interface TaskStopCondition {
100	  readonly maxDurationMs?: number;
101	  readonly maxIterations?: number;
102	  readonly exitCondition?: readonly ConditionTerm[]; // new: early exit when condition met
103	}
104	
105	// --- TaskDefinition (restructured) ---
106	
107	export interface TaskDefinition {
108	  readonly id: string;
109	  readonly name: string;
110	  readonly steps: readonly TaskStepDefinition[];
111	  readonly schedule: ScheduleDriver; // replaces schedulingMode + flat scheduling fields
112	  readonly stopCondition?: TaskStopCondition;
113	  readonly fieldVariations?: readonly FieldVariation[];
114	  readonly errorPolicy: TaskErrorPolicy;
115	}
116	
117	// --- Lifecycle ---
118	
119	export const TASK_LIFECYCLE_STATUSES = [
120	  'created',
121	  'running',
122	  'paused',
123	  'stopped',
124	  'completed',
125	  'failed',
126	] as const;
127	export type TaskLifecycleStatus = (typeof TASK_LIFECYCLE_STATUSES)[number];
128	
129	export type LifecycleAction = 'start' | 'pause' | 'resume' | 'stop' | 'complete' | 'fail';
130	
131	// --- Step results ---
132	
133	export interface SendStepResult {
134	  readonly kind: 'send';
135	  readonly sendResult: SendResult;
136	}
137	
138	export interface WaitConditionStepResult {
139	  readonly kind: 'wait-condition';
140	  readonly matched: boolean;
141	  readonly matchedValue?: number | string;
142	  readonly timedOut: boolean;
143	}
144	
145	export interface DelayStepResult {
146	  readonly kind: 'delay';
147	  readonly completed: boolean;
148	}
149	
150	export type TaskStepResultPayload =
151	  | SendStepResult
152	  | WaitConditionStepResult
153	  | DelayStepResult;
154	
155	export interface TaskStepResult extends TaskStepResultPayload {
156	  readonly stepIndex: number;
157	  readonly iteration: number;
158	  readonly appliedPolicy?: TaskErrorAction;
159	}
160	
161	// --- Instance state ---
162	
163	export interface TaskInstanceState {
164	  readonly instanceId: string;
165	  readonly definitionRef: TaskDefinition;
166	  readonly lifecycle: TaskLifecycleStatus;
167	  readonly startedAt?: string;
168	  readonly pausedAt?: string;
169	  readonly stoppedAt?: string;
170	  readonly completedAt?: string;
171	  readonly failedAt?: string;
172	  readonly currentStepIndex: number;
173	  readonly currentIteration: number;
174	  readonly stepResults: readonly TaskStepResult[];
175	  readonly error?: string;
176	}
177	
178	export type ReadonlyTaskInstanceState = ReadonlyDeep<TaskInstanceState>;
179	
180	// --- Progress ---
181	
182	export interface TaskProgress {
183	  readonly stepsTotal: number;
184	  readonly stepsCompleted: number;
185	  readonly stepsFailed: number;
186	  readonly stepsSkipped: number;
187	  readonly iterationsCompleted: number;
188	  readonly iterationsTotal: number | null;
189	  readonly elapsedMs: number;
190	  readonly estimatedRemainingMs: number | null;
191	  readonly lastStepResult: TaskStepResult | null;
192	}
193	
194	// --- Execution summary ---
195	
196	export interface TaskExecutionSummary {
197	  readonly kind: 'completed' | 'stopped' | 'failed';
198	  readonly summary: {
199	    readonly stepsTotal: number;
200	    readonly stepsSucceeded: number;
201	    readonly stepsFailed: number;
202	    readonly stepsSkipped: number;
203	  };
204	  readonly durationMs: number;
205	  readonly startedAt: string;
206	  readonly finishedAt: string;
207	}
208	
209	// --- Condition match input ---
210	// Decoupled from receive internals. Service layer maps from receive public API outputs.
211	
212	export interface ConditionMatchInput {
213	  readonly frameId: string;
214	  readonly fieldValues: Readonly<Record<string, number | string | null>>;
215	  readonly sourceId?: string;
216	}
217	
218	// --- ResolvedStopCondition & resolveStopCondition ---
219	
220	export type ResolvedStopCondition = TaskStopCondition;
221	
222	export function resolveStopCondition(def: TaskDefinition): ResolvedStopCondition {
223	  if (def.fieldVariations && def.fieldVariations.length > 0) {
224	    const maxLen = Math.max(...def.fieldVariations.map(v => v.values.length));
225	    return { ...def.stopCondition, maxIterations: maxLen };
226	  }
227	  // Immediate schedule defaults to 1 iteration
228	  if (def.schedule.kind === 'immediate' && !def.stopCondition?.maxIterations) {
229	    return { ...def.stopCondition, maxIterations: 1 };
230	  }
231	  return def.stopCondition ?? {};
232	}
233	
```

```
1	import type {
2	  TaskDefinition,
3	  TaskInstanceState,
4	  TaskProgress,
5	} from '../core';
6	import { isTerminal } from '../core';
7	import type { SendServiceProvider, ReceiveEventSource } from '../adapters';
8	import type { TaskStateSnapshot, TaskStatisticsSnapshot } from '../state';
9	import { createTaskState, type TaskStateContainer } from '../state/task-state';
10	import {
11	  selectTaskInstance,
12	  selectTaskProgress,
13	  selectTaskSnapshot,
14	  selectTaskStatistics,
15	} from '../selectors';
16	import { ConditionRegistry } from './condition-registry';
17	import { createStepExecutors } from './task-step-executors';
18	import { createLifecycleManager } from './ta[REDACTED_SK]';
19	import { createErrorPolicyHandler } from './task-error-policy';
20	import { createIterationLoops } from './task-iteration-loops';
21	
22	// --- Public interfaces ---
23	
24	export interface TaskReader {
25	  getSnapshot(): TaskStateSnapshot;
26	  getInstance(instanceId: string): TaskInstanceState | undefined;
27	  getProgress(instanceId: string): TaskProgress | undefined;
28	  getStatistics(): TaskStatisticsSnapshot;
29	}
30	
31	export interface TaskService extends TaskReader {
32	  createTask(definition: TaskDefinition): TaskInstanceState;
33	  startTask(instanceId: string): void;
34	  pauseTask(instanceId: string): void;
35	  resumeTask(instanceId: string): void;
36	  stopTask(instanceId: string): void;
37	  stopAll(): number;
38	  removeTask(instanceId: string): void;
39	  retryTask(sourceInstanceId: string): TaskInstanceState | undefined;
40	  updateTask(instanceId: string, definition: TaskDefinition): TaskInstanceState | undefined;
41	  onSettled(instanceId: string): Promise<void>;
42	}
43	
44	export interface CreateTaskServiceOptions {
45	  readonly sendService: SendServiceProvider;
46	  readonly receiveEventSource: ReceiveEventSource;
47	  readonly timerService?: unknown; // placeholder for future TimerService injection
48	  readonly fieldValueProvider?: () => Readonly<Record<string, number | string | null>>;
49	  readonly state?: TaskStateContainer;
50	  readonly now?: () => string;
51	}
52	
53	// --- Factory ---
54	
55	let nextInstanceId = 1;
56	
57	export function createTaskService(options: CreateTaskServiceOptions): TaskService {
58	  const sendService = options.sendService;
59	  const receiveSource = options.receiveEventSource;
60	  const state = options.state ?? createTaskState();
61	  const now = options.now ?? (() => new Date().toISOString());
62	  const fieldValueProvider = options.fieldValueProvider;
63	
64	  // Execution control
65	  const abortResolvers = new Map<string, () => void>();
66	  const settleResolvers = new Map<string, () => void>();
67	
68	  // Condition matching
69	  const conditionRegistry = new ConditionRegistry();
70	  let receiveSubscription: (() => void) | null = null;
71	  let subscriptionRefCount = 0;
72	
73	  // Wire sub-modules
74	  const lifecycle = createLifecycleManager({
75	    state,
76	    conditionRegistry,
77	    abortResolvers,
78	    settleResolvers,
79	    now,
80	  });
81	
82	  const stepExecutors = createStepExecutors({
83	    state,
84	    sendService,
85	    conditionRegistry,
86	    fieldValueProvider,
87	    now,
88	  });
89	
90	  const errorPolicy = createErrorPolicyHandler({
91	    state,
92	    conditionRegistry,
93	    updateLifecycle: lifecycle.updateLifecycle,
94	    stepExecutors,
95	    now,
96	  });
97	
98	  const loops = createIterationLoops({
99	    state,
100	    conditionRegistry,
101	    updateLifecycle: lifecycle.updateLifecycle,
102	    stepExecutors,
103	    errorPolicy,
104	    fieldValueProvider,
105	    now,
106	  });
107	
108	  // --- Subscription management ---
109	
110	  function ensureSubscription(): void {
111	    if (receiveSubscription) {
112	      subscriptionRefCount++;
113	      return;
114	    }
115	    receiveSubscription = receiveSource.subscribe((input) => {
116	      conditionRegistry.processInput(input);
117	    });
118	    subscriptionRefCount = 1;
119	  }
120	
121	  function releaseSubscription(): void {
122	    subscriptionRefCount--;
123	    if (subscriptionRefCount <= 0 && receiveSubscription) {
124	      receiveSubscription();
125	      receiveSubscription = null;
126	      subscriptionRefCount = 0;
127	    }
128	  }
129	
130	  // --- Execution loop orchestration ---
131	
132	  async function runExecutionLoop(
133	    instanceId: string,
134	    signal: Promise<void>,
135	  ): Promise<void> {
136	    const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
137	    if (!inst) return;
138	
139	    try {
140	      ensureSubscription();
141	
142	      // Use the unified runTask via the loops context
143	      await loops.runTimedLoop(instanceId, inst.definitionRef, signal);
144	
145	      const current = state.getInstance(instanceId);
146	      if (current && current.lifecycle === 'running') {
147	        lifecycle.completeTask(instanceId);
148	      }
149	    } catch (err) {
150	      const current = state.getInstance(instanceId);
151	      if (current && current.lifecycle === 'running') {
152	        lifecycle.failTask(instanceId, err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));
153	      }
154	    } finally {
155	      releaseSubscription();
156	      abortResolvers.delete(instanceId);
157	    }
158	  }
159	
160	  // --- Public API ---
161	
162	  return {
163	    getSnapshot() {
164	      return selectTaskSnapshot(state.getSnapshot());
165	    },
166	
167	    getInstance(instanceId) {
168	      return selectTaskInstance(state.getSnapshot(), instanceId);
169	    },
170	
171	    getProgress(instanceId) {
172	      return selectTaskProgress(state.getSnapshot(), instanceId);
173	    },
174	
175	    getStatistics() {
176	      return selectTaskStatistics(state.getSnapshot());
177	    },
178	
179	    createTask(definition) {
180	      const instanceId = `task-inst-${nextInstanceId++}`;
181	      return state.createInstance(instanceId, definition);
182	    },
183	
184	    startTask(instanceId) {
185	      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
186	      if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'created') return;
187	
188	      lifecycle.updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'start');
189	
190	      const signal = lifecycle.createAbortSignal(instanceId);
191	
192	      // Fire-and-forget execution loop
193	      runExecutionLoop(instanceId, signal);
194	    },
195	
196	    pauseTask(instanceId) {
197	      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
198	      if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'running') return;
199	
200	      lifecycle.updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'pause');
201	      conditionRegistry.clear();
202	      lifecycle.abortInstance(instanceId);
203	    },
204	
205	    resumeTask(instanceId) {
206	      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
207	      if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'paused') return;
208	
209	      lifecycle.updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'resume');
210	
211	      const signal = lifecycle.createAbortSignal(instanceId);
212	      runExecutionLoop(instanceId, signal);
213	    },
214	
215	    stopTask(instanceId) {
216	      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
217	      if (!inst) return;
218	      if (inst.lifecycle !== 'running' && inst.lifecycle !== 'paused') return;
219	
220	      lifecycle.updateLifecycle(instanceId, 'stop');
221	      conditionRegistry.clear();
222	      lifecycle.abortInstance(instanceId);
223	      lifecycle.resolveSettle(instanceId);
224	    },
225	
226	    stopAll(): number {
227	      const snapshot = state.getSnapshot();
228	      let stoppedCount = 0;
229	      for (const inst of snapshot.instances) {
230	        if (inst.lifecycle === 'running' || inst.lifecycle === 'paused') {
231	          lifecycle.updateLifecycle(inst.instanceId, 'stop');
232	          conditionRegistry.clear();
233	          lifecycle.abortInstance(inst.instanceId);
234	          lifecycle.resolveSettle(inst.instanceId);
235	          stoppedCount++;
236	        }
237	      }
238	      return stoppedCount;
239	    },
240	
241	    removeTask(instanceId) {
242	      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
243	      if (!inst) return;
244	      if (!isTerminal(inst.lifecycle)) return;
245	
246	      lifecycle.abortInstance(instanceId);
247	      state.removeInstance(instanceId);
248	    },
249	
250	    retryTask(sourceInstanceId) {
251	      const source = state.getInstance(sourceInstanceId);
252	      if (!source || !isTerminal(source.lifecycle)) return undefined;
253	
254	      const newInstanceId = `task-inst-${nextInstanceId++}`;
255	      state.createInstance(newInstanceId, source.definitionRef);
256	      lifecycle.updateLifecycle(newInstanceId, 'start');
257	      const signal = lifecycle.createAbortSignal(newInstanceId);
258	      runExecutionLoop(newInstanceId, signal);
259	      return state.getInstance(newInstanceId);
260	    },
261	
262	    updateTask(instanceId, definition) {
263	      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
264	      if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'created') return undefined;
265	      return state.updateInstance(instanceId, { definitionRef: definition });
266	    },
267	
268	    async onSettled(instanceId) {
269	      const inst = state.getInstance(instanceId);
270	      if (!inst || isTerminal(inst.lifecycle)) return;
271	
272	      await new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
273	        settleResolvers.set(instanceId, resolve);
274	      });
275	    },
276	  };
277	}
278	
```

```
1	import type { TaskDefinition, TaskStepResult, ScheduleDriver, FieldVariation, ResolvedStopCondition } from '../core';
2	import { evaluateConditionGroup, resolveStopCondition } from '../core';
3	import type { TaskStateContainer } from '../state/task-state';
4	import type { ConditionRegistry } from './condition-registry';
5	import type { UpdateLifecycleFn } from './ta[REDACTED_SK]';
6	import type { createStepExecutors } from './task-step-executors';
7	import { sleep } from './task-step-executors';
8	import type { createErrorPolicyHandler } from './task-error-policy';
9	
10	// --- Task execution context ---
11	
12	export interface TaskExecutionContext {
13	  readonly definition: TaskDefinition;
14	  readonly resolvedStop: ResolvedStopCondition;
15	  state: TaskStateContainer;
16	  stepExecutors: ReturnType<typeof createStepExecutors>;
17	  conditionRegistry: ConditionRegistry;
18	  updateLifecycle: UpdateLifecycleFn;
19	  errorPolicy: ReturnType<typeof createErrorPolicyHandler>;
20	  fieldValueProvider?: () => Readonly<Record<string, number | string | null>>;
21	  now: () => string;
22	}
23	
24	// --- ScheduleDriverAdapter ---
25	
26	interface ScheduleDriverAdapter {
27	  waitForNext(instanceId: string, iteration: number, signal: Promise<void>): Promise<boolean>;
28	  dispose(): void;
29	}
30	
31	function createDriver(
32	  schedule: ScheduleDriver,
33	  ctx: TaskExecutionContext,
34	  instanceId: string,
35	  signal: Promise<void>,
36	): ScheduleDriverAdapter {
37	  switch (schedule.kind) {
38	    case 'immediate':
39	      return { waitForNext: async () => true, dispose: () => {} };
40	
41	    case 'timer':
42	      return createTimerDriver(schedule.intervalMs);
43	
44	    case 'event':
45	      return createEventDriver(schedule, ctx, instanceId, signal);
46	  }
47	}
48	
49	function createTimerDriver(intervalMs: number): ScheduleDriverAdapter {
50	  let firstCall = true;
51	
52	  return {
53	    async waitForNext(_id, _iter, signal) {
54	      if (firstCall) { firstCall = false; return true; }
55	
56	      const delay = sleep(intervalMs);
57	      const race = await Promise.race([
58	        delay.promise.then(() => true),
59	        signal.then(() => false),
60	      ]);
61	      if (!race) delay.cancel();
62	      return race;
63	    },
64	    dispose() {},
65	  };
66	}
67	
68	function createEventDriver(
69	  schedule: Extract<ScheduleDriver, { kind: 'event' }>,
70	  ctx: TaskExecutionContext,
71	  _instanceId: string,
72	  signal: Promise<void>,
73	): ScheduleDriverAdapter {
74	  const { conditions, cooldownMs = 0 } = schedule;
75	  let lastTriggerTime = 0;
76	  let notifyResolve: ((value: boolean) => void) | null = null;
77	  let disposed = false;
78	
79	  const group = ctx.conditionRegistry.registerGroup(conditions, () => {
80	    if (disposed) return;
81	    const now = Date.now();
82	    if (cooldownMs > 0 && lastTriggerTime > 0 && now - lastTriggerTime < cooldownMs) return;
83	    lastTriggerTime = now;
84	    if (notifyResolve) { notifyResolve(true); notifyResolve = null; }
85	  });
86	
87	  return {
88	    async waitForNext() {
89	      if (disposed) return false;
90	      return Promise.race([
91	        new Promise<boolean>((resolve) => { notifyResolve = resolve; }),
92	        signal.then(() => false),
93	      ]);
94	    },
95	    dispose() {
96	      disposed = true;
97	      ctx.conditionRegistry.unregisterGroup(group);
98	      if (notifyResolve) { notifyResolve(false); notifyResolve = null; }
99	    },
100	  };
101	}
102	
103	// --- StopGuard ---
104	
105	interface StopGuard {
106	  shouldStop(instanceId: string, iteration: number): boolean;
107	  checkExitCondition(): boolean;
108	}
109	
110	function createStopGuard(stop: ResolvedStopCondition, ctx: TaskExecutionContext): StopGuard {
111	  const startTime = Date.now();
112	
113	  return {
114	    shouldStop(instanceId, iteration) {
115	      const inst = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId);
116	      if (!inst || inst.lifecycle !== 'running') return true;
117	      if (stop.maxIterations !== undefined && iteration >= stop.maxIterations) return true;
118	      if (stop.maxDurationMs !== undefined && Date.now() - startTime >= stop.maxDurationMs) return true;
119	      return false;
120	    },
121	
122	    checkExitCondition() {
123	      if (!stop.exitCondition) return false;
124	      if (!ctx.fieldValueProvider) return false;
125	      return evaluateConditionGroup(stop.exitCondition, ctx.fieldValueProvider());
126	    },
127	  };
128	}
129	
130	// --- fieldVariations injection ---
131	
132	function resolveFieldValues(
133	  baseValues: Readonly<Record<string, string | number | boolean>> | undefined,
134	  fieldVariations: readonly FieldVariation[] | undefined,
135	  iteration: number,
136	): Readonly<Record<string, string | number | boolean>> {
137	  if (!fieldVariations || fieldVariations.length === 0) return baseValues ?? {};
138	
139	  const result: Record<string, string | number | boolean> = { ...(baseValues ?? {}) };
140	  for (const variation of fieldVariations) {
141	    if (iteration < variation.values.length) {
142	      result[variation.fieldId] = variation.values[iteration]!;
143	    }
144	  }
145	  return result;
146	}
147	
148	// --- executeRepeatableSend ---
149	
150	async function executeRepeatableSend(
151	  ctx: TaskExecutionContext,
152	  instanceId: string,
153	  step: Extract<TaskDefinition['steps'][number], { kind: 'send' }>,
154	  iteration: number,
155	  stepIndex: number,
156	  definition: TaskDefinition,
157	  signal: Promise<void>,
158	): Promise<boolean> {
159	  const { repeat } = step.config;
160	  if (!repeat) {
161	    const result = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeSendStep(instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex, definition);
162	    ctx.state.addStepResult(instanceId, result);
163	    return result.sendResult.kind === 'sent';
164	  }
165	
166	  let count = 0;
167	  const maxCount = repeat.maxCount ?? Infinity;
168	
169	  while (count < maxCount) {
170	    const result = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeSendStep(instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex, definition);
171	    ctx.state.addStepResult(instanceId, result);
172	
173	    if (result.sendResult.kind !== 'sent') return false;
174	
175	    count++;
176	
177	    if (repeat.until && ctx.fieldValueProvider) {
178	      if (evaluateConditionGroup(repeat.until, ctx.fieldValueProvider())) break;
179	    }
180	
181	    if (count < maxCount) {
182	      const delay = sleep(repeat.intervalMs);
183	      const race = await Promise.race([delay.promise.then(() => true), signal.then(() => false)]);
184	      if (!race) { delay.cancel(); return false; }
185	    }
186	  }
187	
188	  return true;
189	}
190	
191	// --- executeSteps (within an iteration) ---
192	
193	async function executeSteps(
194	  instanceId: string,
195	  iteration: number,
196	  ctx: TaskExecutionContext,
197	  signal: Promise<void>,
198	): Promise<boolean> {
199	  const { steps } = ctx.definition;
200	  const inst = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId);
201	  if (!inst) return false;
202	  const completedInIter = inst.stepResults
203	    .filter((r) => r.iteration === iteration)
204	    .map((r) => r.stepIndex);
205	  const startIndex = completedInIter.length;
206	
207	  for (let i = startIndex; i < steps.length; i++) {
208	    const current = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId);
209	    if (!current || current.lifecycle !== 'running') return false;
210	
211	    ctx.state.updateInstance(instanceId, { currentStepIndex: i });
212	
213	    const step = steps[i]!;
214	
215	    // For send steps with repeat, use executeRepeatableSend
216	    if (step.kind === 'send' && step.config.repeat) {
217	      const success = await executeRepeatableSend(ctx, instanceId, step, iteration, i, ctx.definition, signal);
218	      if (!success) return false;
219	    } else {
220	      const outcome = await executeStepCore(instanceId, step, iteration, i, ctx, signal);
221	      if (outcome === null) return false;
222	      ctx.state.addStepResult(instanceId, outcome.result);
223	      if (outcome.shouldStop) return false;
224	    }
225	
226	    // intervalAfterMs for send steps
227	    if (step.kind === 'send' && step.config.intervalAfterMs) {
228	      const delay = sleep(step.config.intervalAfterMs);
229	      const race = await Promise.race([
230	        delay.promise.then(() => 'done' as const),
231	        signal.then(() => 'interrupted' as const),
232	      ]);
233	      if (race === 'interrupted') {
234	        delay.cancel();
235	        return false;
236	      }
237	    }
238	  }
239	  return true;
240	}
241	
242	// --- executeStepCore ---
243	
244	async function executeStepCore(
245	  instanceId: string,
246	  step: TaskDefinition['steps'][number],
247	  iteration: number,
248	  stepIndex: number,
249	  ctx: TaskExecutionContext,
250	  signal: Promise<void>,
251	): Promise<{ result: TaskStepResult; shouldStop: boolean } | null> {
252	  switch (step.kind) {
253	    case 'send': {
254	      const result = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeSendStep(instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex, ctx.definition);
255	      if (result.sendResult.kind !== 'sent') {
256	        return ctx.errorPolicy.applyErrorPolicy(instanceId, result, ctx.definition.errorPolicy, step, iteration, stepIndex, ctx.definition, signal);
257	      }
258	      return { result, shouldStop: false };
259	    }
260	
261	    case 'wait-condition': {
262	      const { result, interrupted } = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeWaitConditionStep(
263	        instanceId, step, iteration, stepIndex, signal,
264	      );
265	      if (interrupted) return null;
266	
267	      if (!result.matched && result.timedOut) {
268	        switch (step.config.onTimeout) {
269	          case 'continue':
270	            return { result, shouldStop: false };
271	          case 'skip':
272	            return { result: { ...result, appliedPolicy: 'skip-step' }, shouldStop: false };
273	          case 'fail':
274	            return ctx.errorPolicy.applyErrorPolicy(instanceId, result, ctx.definition.errorPolicy, step, iteration, stepIndex, ctx.definition, signal);
275	        }
276	      }
277	      return { result, shouldStop: false };
278	    }
279	
280	    case 'delay': {
281	      const { result, interrupted } = await ctx.stepExecutors.executeDelayStep(
282	        iteration, stepIndex, step.config.durationMs, signal,
283	      );
284	      if (interrupted) return null;
285	      return { result, shouldStop: false };
286	    }
287	  }
288	}
289	
290	// --- runTask (unified loop) ---
291	
292	export async function runTask(
293	  instanceId: string,
294	  ctx: TaskExecutionContext,
295	  signal: Promise<void>,
296	): Promise<void> {
297	  const driver = createDriver(ctx.definition.schedule, ctx, instanceId, signal);
298	  const stopGuard = createStopGuard(ctx.resolvedStop, ctx);
299	
300	  let iteration = ctx.state.getInstance(instanceId)?.currentIteration ?? 0;
301	
302	  while (!stopGuard.shouldStop(instanceId, iteration)) {
303	    const triggered = await driver.waitForNext(instanceId, iteration, signal);
304	    if (!triggered) break;
305	
306	    const completed = await executeSteps(instanceId, iteration, ctx, signal);
307	    if (!completed) break;
308	
309	    if (stopGuard.checkExitCondition()) break;
310	
311	    iteration++;
312	    // Only update iteration if the next iteration might execute
313	    if (!stopGuard.shouldStop(instanceId, iteration)) {
314	      ctx.state.updateInstance(instanceId, { currentIteration: iteration });
315	    }
316	  }
317	
318	  driver.dispose();
319	}
320	
321	// --- Re-export resolveFieldValues for use in buildSendRequest ---
322	
323	export { resolveFieldValues };
324	
325	// --- Legacy interface for backward compatibility during transition ---
326	
327	export interface IterationLoopContext {
328	  state: TaskStateContainer;
329	  conditionRegistry: ConditionRegistry;
330	  updateLifecycle: UpdateLifecycleFn;
331	  stepExecutors: ReturnType<typeof createStepExecutors>;
332	  errorPolicy: ReturnType<typeof createErrorPolicyHandler>;
333	  fieldValueProvider?: () => Readonly<Record<string, number | string | null>>;
334	  now: () => string;
335	}
336	
337	export function createIterationLoops(ctx: IterationLoopContext) {
338	  function buildExecutionContext(definition: TaskDefinition): TaskExecutionContext {
339	    return {
340	      definition,
341	      resolvedStop: resolveStopCondition(definition),
342	      state: ctx.state,
343	      stepExecutors: ctx.stepExecutors,
344	      conditionRegistry: ctx.conditionRegistry,
345	      updateLifecycle: ctx.updateLifecycle,
346	      errorPolicy: ctx.errorPolicy,
347	      fieldValueProvider: ctx.fieldValueProvider,
348	      now: ctx.now,
349	    };
350	  }
351	
352	  async function runTimedLoop(
353	    instanceId: string,
354	    definition: TaskDefinition,
355	    signal: Promise<void>,
356	  ): Promise<void> {
357	    const execCtx = buildExecutionContext(definition);
358	    await runTask(instanceId, execCtx, signal);
359	  }
360	
361	  async function runTriggerLoop(
362	    instanceId: string,
363	    definition: TaskDefinition,
364	    signal: Promise<void>,
365	  ): Promise<void> {
366	    const execCtx = buildExecutionContext(definition);
367	    await runTask(instanceId, execCtx, signal);
368	  }
369	
370	  async function runSequenceLoop(
371	    instanceId: string,
372	    definition: TaskDefinition,
373	    signal: Promise<void>,
374	  ): Promise<void> {
375	    const execCtx = buildExecutionContext(definition);
376	    await runTask(instanceId, execCtx, signal);
377	  }
378	
379	  return { runTimedLoop, runTriggerLoop, runSequenceLoop };
380	}
381
```

```
1	import type { ConditionTerm, ConditionMatchInput } from '../core';
2	import { evaluateConditionGroup } from '../core';
3	
4	interface RegistryGroup {
5	  readonly id: string;
6	  readonly conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[];
7	  readonly onSatisfied: () => void;
8	}
9	
10	export interface ConditionGroup {
11	  readonly _group: RegistryGroup;
12	}
13	
14	export class ConditionRegistry {
15	  private groups: RegistryGroup[] = [];
16	  private frameIndex = new Map<string, Set<number>>();
17	  private nextGroupId = 1;
18	
19	  registerGroup(conditions: readonly ConditionTerm[], onSatisfied: () => void): ConditionGroup {
20	    const id = `cg-${this.nextGroupId++}`;
21	    const group: RegistryGroup = { id, conditions, onSatisfied };
22	    const idx = this.groups.length;
23	    this.groups.push(group);
24	    this.indexGroup(idx, group);
25	    return { _group: group };
26	  }
27	
28	  unregisterGroup(group: ConditionGroup): void {
29	    const idx = this.groups.indexOf(group._group);
30	    if (idx === -1) return;
31	    this.groups.splice(idx, 1);
32	    this.rebuildIndex();
33	  }
34	
35	  processInput(input: ConditionMatchInput): void {
36	    const candidateIndices = this.frameIndex.get(input.frameId);
37	    if (!candidateIndices) return;
38	
39	    for (const idx of candidateIndices) {
40	      const group = this.groups[idx];
41	      if (!group) continue;
42	
43	      // sourceId filter
44	      if (input.sourceId !== undefined) {
45	        const hasSourceMatch = group.conditions.some(
46	          (c) => c.sourceId === undefined || c.sourceId === input.sourceId,
47	        );
48	        if (!hasSourceMatch) continue;
49	      }
50	
51	      if (evaluateConditionGroup(group.conditions, { ...input.fieldValues })) {
52	        group.onSatisfied();
53	      }
54	    }
55	  }
56	
57	  clear(): void {
58	    this.groups = [];
59	    this.frameIndex.clear();
60	  }
61	
62	  get size(): number {
63	    return this.groups.length;
64	  }
65	
66	  private indexGroup(idx: number, group: RegistryGroup): void {
67	    for (const cond of group.conditions) {
68	      if (!this.frameIndex.has(cond.frameId)) {
69	        this.frameIndex.set(cond.frameId, new Set());
70	      }
71	      this.frameIndex.get(cond.frameId)!.add(idx);
72	    }
73	  }
74	
75	  private rebuildIndex(): void {
76	    this.frameIndex.clear();
77	    for (let i = 0; i < this.groups.length; i++) {
78	      this.indexGroup(i, this.groups[i]!);
79	    }
80	  }
81	}
82
```

```
1	import type { TaskService } from '@/features/task';
2	import type { SendService } from '@/features/send';
3	import type { ConnectionService, ConnectionStateSnapshot } from '@/features/connection';
4	import type { FrameAssetReader } from '@/features/frame';
5	import type { ProtocolAdapter } from '../core/protocol-adapter';
6	import type { CommandContext, CommandHandler, CommandResult } from '../core/handler';
7	import type { ScoeCommandFunction, SatelliteConfig, ScoeStatisticsSnapshot, ScoeRuntimeStatus } from '../core/types';
8	import type { CommandIngressStateReader, CommandIngressStateWriter } from '../core/state';
9	import type { ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions } from '../adapters/scoe-protocol-adapter';
10	import type { CommandLogEntry } from '../core/command-log';
11	import type { DataRecorder } from './data-recorder';
12	import { dispatchCommand } from '../core/handler';
13	import { createScoeProtocolAdapter } from '../adapters/scoe-protocol-adapter';

22	import { createCommandLogRecorder } from '../core/command-log';
23	import { createDataRecorder } from './data-recorder';
24	
25	export interface CommandIngressServiceOptions {
26	  readonly globalConfig: ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions['globalConfig'];
27	  readonly commandConfigs: ScoeProtocolAdapterOptions['commandConfigs'];
28	  readonly satelliteConfigs: readonly SatelliteConfig[];
29	  readonly taskService: TaskService;
30	  readonly sendService: SendService;
31	  readonly frameReader: FrameAssetReader;
32	  readonly connectionService: ConnectionService;
33	  readonly connectionSnapshot: () => ConnectionStateSnapshot;
34	  readonly receiveSnapshot: () => unknown;
35	  readonly platformFileReader: (path: string) => Promise<string[]>;
36	  readonly stateReader: CommandIngressStateReader;
37	  readonly stateWriter: CommandIngressStateWriter;
38	}
39	
40	export interface CommandIngressService {
41	  readonly adapter: ProtocolAdapter;
42	  loadSatellite(satelliteId: string): Promise<CommandResult>;
43	  unloadSatellite(): Promise<CommandResult>;
44	  getScoeStatistics(): ScoeStatisticsSnapshot;
45	  getScoeRuntimeStatus(): ScoeRuntimeStatus;
46	  getLoadedSatelliteId(): string;
47	  isScoeFramesLoaded(): boolean;
48	  getCommandLog(): readonly CommandLogEntry[];
49	  clearCommandLog(): void;
50	  getTestDataRecorder(): DataRecorder;
51	  sendTestData(hex: string, connectionId: string): Promise<void>;
52	  dispose(): void;
53	}
54	
55	export function createCommandIngressService(
56	  options: CommandIngressServiceOptions,
57	): CommandIngressService {
58	  const {
59	    satelliteConfigs,
60	    taskService,
61	    sendService,
62	    frameReader,
63	    connectionService,
64	    connectionSnapshot,
65	    receiveSnapshot,
66	    platformFileReader,
67	    stateReader,
68	    stateWriter,
69	  } = options;
70	
71	  // Tracking: connectionId → taskInstanceIds
72	  const trackingMap = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
73	
74	  // C1: Command log recorder
75	  const logRecorder = createCommandLogRecorder();
76	
77	  // C2: Test data recorder
78	  const testDataRecorder = createDataRecorder();
79	
80	  // Handler map
81	  const handlerMap = new Map<ScoeCommandFunction, CommandHandler>([
82	    ['load_satellite_id', createHandleLoadSatelliteId(satelliteConfigs)],
83	    ['unload_satellite_id', handleUnloadSatelliteId],
84	    ['health_check', handleHealthCheck],
85	    ['link_check', handleLinkCheck],
86	    ['send_frame', handleSendFrame],
87	    ['read_file_and_send', handleReadFileAndSend],
88	  ]);
89	
90	  const ctx: CommandContext = {
91	    taskService,
92	    sendService,
93	    frameReader,
94	    connectionService,
95	    connectionSnapshot,
96	    receiveSnapshot,
97	    platformFileReader,
98	    stateReader,
99	    stateWriter,
100	  };
101	
102	  // onCommand: dispatch parsed commands, track tasks, monitor settlement
103	  async function onCommand(parsed: Parameters<typeof dispatchCommand>[0]): Promise<void> {
104	    stateWriter.updateStatus({ lastCommandCode: parsed.commandCode });
105	
106	    const logEntry = logRecorder.record({
107	      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
108	      commandCode: parsed.commandCode,
109	      result: 'pending',
110	    });
111	
112	    const result = await dispatchCommand(parsed, handlerMap, ctx);
113	
114	    if (result.taskId) {
115	      trackTask(parsed.connectionId, result.taskId, parsed, logEntry.id);
116	    } else {
117	      logRecorder.complete(logEntry.id, {
118	        result: result.success ? 'success' : 'error',
119	        error: result.error,
120	      });
121	    }
122	
123	    cleanupDisconnectedConnections();
124	  }
125	
126	  // Create protocol adapter with onCommand dispatch
127	  const adapter = createScoeProtocolAdapter({
128	    globalConfig: options.globalConfig,
129	    commandConfigs: options.commandConfigs,
130	    stateReader,
131	    onCommand,
132	    onConsume: (bytes) => testDataRecorder.record(bytes),
133	  });
134	
135	  // Start status timer (inlined from status-timer.ts)
136	  const timerHandle = setInterval(() => {
137	    stateWriter.tickSecond();
138	  }, 1000);
139	
140	  function trackTask(
141	    connectionId: string,
142	    taskId: string,
143	    command: Parameters<typeof dispatchCommand>[0],
144	    logEntryId: string,
145	  ): void {
146	    let set = trackingMap.get(connectionId);
147	    if (!set) {
148	      set = new Set();
149	      trackingMap.set(connectionId, set);
150	    }
151	    set.add(taskId);
152	
153	    // Single consolidated settlement monitor: cleanup tracking + ack frame + counters
154	    taskService.onSettled(taskId)
155	      .then(async () => {
156	        const inst = taskService.getInstance(taskId);
157	        if (inst?.lifecycle === 'completed') {
158	          try {
159	            await sendAckFrame(command, ctx);
160	          } catch (err) {
161	            // Ack frame send failure — don't crash, just count as error
162	            console.warn('Ack frame send failed', err);
163	          }
164	          stateWriter.incrementSuccessCount();
165	          logRecorder.complete(logEntryId, {
166	            result: 'success',
167	            durationMs: inst.completedAt && inst.startedAt
168	              ? new Date(inst.completedAt).getTime() - new Date(inst.startedAt).getTime()
169	              : undefined,
170	          });
171	        } else {
172	          stateWriter.incrementErrorCount(inst?.error ?? 'task failed');
173	          logRecorder.complete(logEntryId, {
174	            result: 'error',
175	            error: inst?.error ?? 'task failed',
176	          });
177	        }
178	
179	        // Re-lookup Set from trackingMap (avoids stale closure reference)
180	        const current = trackingMap.get(connectionId);
181	        if (!current) return;
182	        current.delete(taskId);
183	        if (current.size === 0) {
184	          trackingMap.delete(connectionId);
185	        }
186	      })
187	      .catch((err) => {
188	        stateWriter.incrementErrorCount(
189	          err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'settlement tracking failed',
190	        );
191	        // Still cleanup tracking
192	        const current = trackingMap.get(connectionId);
193	        if (current) {
194	          current.delete(taskId);
195	          if (current.size === 0) trackingMap.delete(connectionId);
196	        }
197	      });
198	  }
199	
200	  function cleanupDisconnectedConnections(): void {
201	    for (const [connId, taskIds] of trackingMap) {
202	      const fact = connectionService.getConnectionFact(connId);
203	      if (!fact || fact.lifecycle === 'disconnected') {
204	        for (const taskId of taskIds) {
205	          try {
206	            taskService.stopTask(taskId);
207	          } catch (err) {
208	            // Task may already be settled
209	            console.warn('Failed to stop task during cleanup', taskId, err);
210	          }
211	        }
212	        trackingMap.delete(connId);
213	      }
214	    }
215	  }
216	
217	  function dispose(): void {
218	    clearInterval(timerHandle);
219	    for (const [, taskIds] of trackingMap) {
220	      for (const taskId of taskIds) {
221	        try {
222	          taskService.stopTask(taskId);
223	        } catch (err) {
224	          // Ignore errors during cleanup
225	          console.warn('Failed to stop task during dispose', taskId, err);
226	        }
227	      }
228	    }
229	    trackingMap.clear();
230	  }
231	
232	  // CI-R2: delegate loadSatellite to handler logic
233	  async function loadSatellite(satelliteId: string): Promise<CommandResult> {
234	    if (stateReader.loadedSatelliteId !== '') {
235	      return { success: false, error: 'Satellite already loaded' };
236	    }
237	    const satConfig = satelliteConfigs.find((s) => s.satelliteId === satelliteId);
238	    if (!satConfig) {
239	      return { success: false, error: `Unknown satellite ID: ${satelliteId}` };
240	    }
241	    stateWriter.setLoaded(satelliteId, satConfig.commandConfigs);
242	    return { success: true };
243	  }
244	
245	  async function unloadSatellite(): Promise<CommandResult> {
246	    if (!stateReader.scoeFramesLoaded) {
247	      return { success: false, error: 'No satellite loaded' };
248	    }
249	    stateWriter.resetRuntimeState();
250	    return { success: true };
251	  }
252	
253	  // CI-R5: Reader methods replacing deleted selectors
254	  function getScoeStatistics(): ScoeStatisticsSnapshot {
255	    const s = stateReader.getSnapshot();
256	    return {
257	      commandReceiveCount: s.commandReceiveCount,
258	      commandSuccessCount: s.commandSuccessCount,
259	      commandErrorCount: s.commandErrorCount,
260	      runtimeSeconds: s.runtimeSeconds,
261	      satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds: s.satelliteIdRuntimeSeconds,
262	      lastErrorReason: s.lastErrorReason,
263	    };
264	  }
265	
266	  function getScoeRuntimeStatus(): ScoeRuntimeStatus {
267	    const s = stateReader.getSnapshot();
268	    return {
269	      loadedSatelliteId: s.loadedSatelliteId,
270	      scoeFramesLoaded: s.scoeFramesLoaded,
271	      healthStatus: s.healthStatus,
272	      linkTestResult: s.linkTestResult,
273	      lastCommandCode: s.lastCommandCode,
274	      receiveCommandSuccess: s.receiveCommandSuccess,
275	    };
276	  }
277	
278	  function getLoadedSatelliteId(): string {
279	    return stateReader.loadedSatelliteId;
280	  }
281	
282	  function isScoeFramesLoaded(): boolean {
283	    return stateReader.scoeFramesLoaded;
284	  }
285	
286	  function getCommandLog(): readonly CommandLogEntry[] {
287	    return logRecorder.getAll();
288	  }
289	
290	  function clearCommandLog(): void {
291	    logRecorder.clear();
292	  }
293	
294	  function getTestDataRecorder(): DataRecorder {
295	    return testDataRecorder;
296	  }
297	
298	  async function sendTestData(hex: string, connectionId: string): Promise<void> {
299	    const bytes = hexToBytes(hex);
300	    await connectionService.write({ connectionId, bytes });
301	  }
302	
303	  return {
304	    adapter,
305	    loadSatellite,
306	    unloadSatellite,
307	    getScoeStatistics,
308	    getScoeRuntimeStatus,
309	    getLoadedSatelliteId,
310	    isScoeFramesLoaded,
311	    getCommandLog,
312	    clearCommandLog,
313	    getTestDataRecorder,
314	    sendTestData,
315	    dispose,
316	  };
317	}
318	
319
```

```
1	import type { CommandHandler } from '../core/handler';
2	import { buildSendFrameTask } from '../core/task-builder';
3	
4	export const handleSendFrame: CommandHandler = async (command, ctx) => {
5	  if (!ctx.stateReader.scoeFramesLoaded) {
6	    return { success: false, error: 'Satellite not loaded' };
7	  }
8	
9	  const taskDef = buildSendFrameTask(command);
10	  const instance = ctx.taskService.createTask(taskDef);
11	  ctx.taskService.startTask(instance.instanceId);
12	
13	  ctx.stateWriter.incrementReceiveCount();
14	
15	  return { success: true, taskId: instance.instanceId };
16	};
17
```

```
1	import type { ParsedCommand } from '../core/protocol-adapter';
2	import type { CommandContext } from '../core/handler';
3	
4	/**
5	 * Send acknowledgment frame via sendService after task completes successfully.
6	 * Uses successFrameId from command config (falling back to global config),
7	 * resolved through sendService.execute() which handles frame serialization
8	 * and connection write.
9	 */
10	export async function sendAckFrame(
11	  command: ParsedCommand,
12	  ctx: CommandContext,
13	): Promise<void> {
14	  const successFrameId =
15	    command.commandConfig.successFrameId ??
16	    ctx.stateReader.globalConfig().successFrameId;
17	  if (!successFrameId) return;
18	
19	  const frame = ctx.frameReader.getFrame(successFrameId);
20	  if (!frame) return;
21	
22	  await ctx.sendService.execute({
23	    frameId: successFrameId,
24	    targetId:
25	      command.commandConfig.frameMappings?.[0]?.targetId ||
26	      ctx.stateReader.globalConfig().udpTargetId,
27	    userFieldValues: {},
28	  });
29	}
30
```
